cols=range(5)\nexist = set()\nfor r in range(7):\n    for c in range(5):\n        pass\nexisting_cells = [(0,1),(0,2),(0,3),(1,1),(1,3),(2,0),(2,1),(2,2),(2,3),(2,4),(3,0),(3,2),(3,4),(4,0),(4,1),(4,2),(4,3),(4,4),(5,1),(5,3),(6,1),(6,2),(6,3)]\ndef transforms(M):\n    out=[]\n    cur=[row[:] for row in M]\n    for k in range(4):\n        out.append([row[:] for row in cur])\n        out.append([[cur[2-j][i] for j in range(3)] for i in range(3)])  # transpose of rotated\n        cur=[[cur[2-j][i] for j in range(3)] for i in range(3)]\n    uniq=[]\n    seen=set()\n    for m in out:\n        t=tuple(tuple(r) for r in m)\n        if t not in seen: seen.add(t);           "code": "def sim(plan, speeds=(4.0,5.0,5.7,6.5), pieces0=None, inv0=None):\n    # pieces: dict name->(color,(r,c) center). inv: list of centers\n    import math\n    def cells_p(color, ctr):\n        r,c = ctr\n        if color=='M': rr=(r-0.5,r+0.5); cc=(c-0.5,c+0.5)\n        elif color=='p': rr=(r-1,r+1); cc=(c-1,c+1)\n        else: rr=(r-1.5,r+1.5); cc=(c-1.5,c+1.5)\n        return (math.ceil(rr[0]),math.floor(rr[1]),math.ceil(cc[0]),math.floor(cc[1]))\n    def cells_i(ctr):\n        r,c = ctr\n        return (math.ceil(r-1.5),math.floor(r+2.5),math.ceil(c-2),math.floor(c+2))\n    def hit(a,b): return not (a[1]<b[0] or b[1]<a[0] or a[3]<b[2] or b[3]<a[2])\n    out=[]\n    for sp in speeds:\n        P = {k:(v[0],(v[1][0],v[1][1])) for k,v in pieces0.items()}\n        I = [list(x) for x in inv0]\n        dead=None\n        for i,(tgt,kind) in enumerate(plan):\n            # apply action: kind 'move' -> nearest piece within 8 of tgt moves to tgt; 'merge' -> two same-color within 8 merge\n            names = [k for k in P]\n            d = {k: math.hypot(P[k][1][0]-tgt[0], P[k][1][1]-tgt[1]) for k in names}\n            inr = [k for k in names if d[k]<=8.0]\n            if kind=='merge':\n                cols = {}\n                for k in inr: cols.setdefault(P[k][0],[]).append(k)\n                pick = [v for v in cols.values() if len(v)>=2]\n                if not pick: out.append((sp,i,'nomerge')); dead='model'; break\n                grp = sorted(pick[0], key=lambda k:(-{'Y':3,'p':2,'M':1}[P[k][0]], k))[:2]\n                up = {'M':'p','p':'Y'}[P[grp[0]][0]]\n                for k in grp: del P[k]\n                P['n%d'%i] = (up,(tgt[0],tgt[1]))\n            else:\n                if not inr: out.append((sp,i,'noreach')); dead='model'; break\n                k = min(inr, key=lambda k:d[k])\n                P[k] = (P[k][0],(tgt[0],tgt[1]))\n            # invader moves toward nearest piece\n            for iv in I:\n                if not P: break\n                k = min(P, key=lambda k: math.hypot(P[k][1][0]-iv[0], P[k][1][1]-iv[1]))\n                t = P[k][1]; dist = math.hypot(t[0]-iv[0], t[1]-iv[1]) or 1\n                iv[0] += round(sp*(t[0]-iv[0])/dist,2); iv[1] += round(sp*(t[1]-iv[1])/dist,2)\n            # contact check\n            for iv in I:\n                ic = cells_i(iv)\n                for k,v in list(P.items()):\n                    if hit(ic, cells_p(v[0], v[1])):\n                        out.append((sp,i,'killed',k,[round(x,1) for x in iv])); dead='dead'; break\n                if dead: break\n            if dead: break\n        if not dead:\n            last = list(P.values())[0]\n            out.append((sp,'survived',len(P),last[0],last[1]))\n    return out\npieces0 = {'a':('p',(22,11)), 'b':('p',(22,47))}\ninv0 = [(30.5,11),(30.5,47)]\nplan = [((18,17),'move'),((18,41),'move'),((16,24),'move'),((16,40),'move'),((15,32),'merge'),((14,33),'move')]\nfor r in sim(plan,pieces0=pieces0,inv0=inv0): print(r)\n"

        "arguments": {
          "code": "\ndef analyze(frame):\n    seg = frame.segmentation\n    ns = seg['nodes']\n    grid_rows=[4,12,20,28,36,44,52]; grid_cols=[12,20,28,36,44]\n    pieces={}; cells={}\n    for n in ns:\n        b=n['boundary']; rs=[p[0] for p in b]; cs=[p[1] for p in b]\n        r0,r1,c0,c1=min(rs),max(rs),min(cs),max(cs)\n        if n['pixels']<36 and (r1-r0)<=5 and (c1-c0)<=5 and r0>=10:\n            for r in range(r0,r1+1):\n                for c in range(c0,c1+1): pieces[(r,c)]=n['color']\n        if n['pixels']==36 and r1-r0==5 and c1-c0==5:\n            cells[(grid_rows.index(r0),grid_cols.index(c0))]=n['color']\n    pats=[(12,28),(28,20),(28,36),(44,28)]\n    A={}\n    for (pr,pc) in pats:\n        center=pieces[(pr+2,pc+2)]; other='R' if center=='O' else 'O'\n        R=grid_rows.index(pr); C=grid_cols.index(pc)\n        for i in range(3):\n            for j in range(3):\n                if (i,j)==(1,1): continue\n                s=pieces[(pr+2*i,pc+2*j)]\n                A[(R-1+i,C-1+j)] = center if s=='W' else other\n    board = {k:v for k,v in cells.items()}\n    return board, A\n\n# find level-3 frames\nlv3 = [h for h in history if h.frame.level==3]\nprint('level3 entries', len(lv3))\nfor i,h in enumerate(lv3):\n    board,A = analyze(h.frame)\n    match = all(board.get(k)==A.get(k) for k in A)\n    print(i, h.action, 'step',h.frame.step, 'board==A:', match, 'orange', sum(1 for v in board.values() if v=='O'))\n"

          "code": "\nimport itertools\nLEG = {1:['WWW','W.g','gWg'], 2:['gWg','g.W','WWg'], 3:['gWW','W.g','gWg'], 4:['gWg','W.W','WWW']}\nDOT = {1:'O',2:'R',3:'R',4:'O'}\nPOS = {1:(1,2),2:(3,1),3:(3,3),4:(5,2)}\nCELLS=[(0,1),(0,2),(0,3),(1,1),(1,3),(2,0),(2,1),(2,2),(2,3),(2,4),(3,0),(3,2),(3,4),\n       (4,0),(4,1),(4,2),(4,3),(4,4),(5,1),(5,3),(6,1),(6,2),(6,3)]\ndef rot(M,k):\n    g=[list(r) for r in M]\n    for _ in range(k): g=[list(r) for r in zip(*g[::-1])]\n    return [''.join(r) for r in g]\ndef board_for(combo, invert):\n    out={}\n    for idx,(rotk,pol) in zip([1,2,3,4],combo):\n        t=rot(LEG[idx],rotk); R,C=POS[idx]\n        dot=DOT[idx]; other='R' if dot=='O' else 'O'\n        wcol = dot if not pol else other\n        gcol = other if not pol else dot\n        for i in range(3):\n            for j in range(3):\n                if (i,j)==(1,1): continue\n                s=t[i][j]\n                key=(R-1+i,C-1+j)\n                col = wcol if s=='W' else gcol\n                if key in out and out[key]!=col: return None\n                out[key]=col\n    if len(out)!=len(CELLS): return None\n    return ''.join(out[c] for c in CELLS)\nfor polset in ([0],[1],[0,1]):\n    sols=set()\n    for combo in itertools.product(itertools.product(range(4),polset),repeat=4):\n        b=board_for(combo, False)\n        if b: sols.add(b)\n    print('polarity options',polset,'-> distinct boards:',len(sols))\n    for s in sorted(sols)[:8]:\n        print('   ',s[:23])\n"
        },

[{\\\"action\\\": \\\"MOUSE\\\", \\\"row\\\": r0 + 3, \\\"col\\\": c0 + 3}],\\n            \\\"evidence_pixels\\\": ev, \\\"replaces\\\": names,\\n            \\\"reason\\\": \\\"observed clicks changed exactly the 36 pixels of one 6x6 \\\"\\n                      \\\"cell to the clicked cell's base colour plus one bottom-row \\\"\\n                      \\\"counter pixel; the mixed cell is the only unexplored role \\\"\\n                      \\\"(pattern rotation vs cell toggle) and one click settles it\\\"}\\ndef predict(frame, action):\\n    g = grid_of(frame)\\n    if action.get(\\\"action\\\") != \\\"MOUSE\\\" or len(g) < 3:\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"unknown\\\", \\\"reason\\\": \\\"not a MOUSE action or no grid\\\"}\\n    r = action.get(\\\"row\\\")\\n    c = action.get(\\\"col\\\")\\n    if r is None or c is None:\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"unknown\\\", \\\"reason\\\": \\\"action lacks row/col\\\"}\\n    if not (0 <= r < len(g) and 0 <= c < len(g[r])):\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"unknown\\\", \\\"reason\\\": \\\"click outside grid\\\"}\\n    ch = g[r][c]\\n    if ch == \\\"c\\\":\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"noop\\\", \\\"action\\\": action,\\n                \\\"object_pixels\\\": [[r, c, ch]], \\\"target_pixels\\\": [[r, c, ch]],\\n                \\\"expected_pixels\\\": [[r, c, ch]],\\n                \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"click on background gap, no task cell there\\\",\\n                \\\"scope\\\": \\\"no cell or counter pixel should change\\\"}\\n    r0 = r - 2\\n    c0 = c - 2\\n    pts = cell6(g, r0, c0)\\n    obj = [[a, b, g[a][b]] for a, b in pts]\\n    if ch in \\\"RO\\\":\\n        nxt = \\\"O\\\" if ch == \\\"R\\\" else \\\"R\\\"\\n        exp = [[a, b, nxt] for a, b in pts]\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"change\\\", \\\"action\\\": action,\\n                \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj, \\\"target_pixels\\\": obj,\\n                \\\"expected_pixels\\\": exp,\\n                \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"cell containing %d,%d is uniform %s and a click \\\"\\n                                \\\"repaints all 36 of its pixels to %s\\\"\\n                                % (r, c, ch, nxt),\\n                \\\"scope\\\": \\\"the whole clicked 6x6 cell flips base colour; other \\\"\\n                         \\\"cells and legend cells keep their pixels\\\"}\\n    exp = [[a, b, g[a][b]] for a, b in pts]\\n    return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"noop\\\", \\\"action\\\": action,\\n            \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj, \\\"target_pixels\\\": [[r, c, ch]],\\n            \\\"expected_pixels\\\": exp,\\n            \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"cell containing %d,%d is a W/g/R pattern, not a \\\"\\n                            \\\"toggleable R/O cell, so its 36 pixels stay as-is\\\"\\n                            % (r, c),\\n            \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pattern cell rows %d-%d cols %d-%d unchanged; distinguishes \\\"\\n                     \\\"legend-role from cell-role\\\" % (r0, r0 + 5, c0, c0 + 5)}\\n\"}"

          "code": "Rule = \"Rule for level 4: Goal = build yellow 4x4 and center it exactly on the ball center. A pink invader chases the nearest piece and destroys it on contact (refund is one tier down); UNDO reverts both the loss and the invader move. pc[0], cc - pc[1])\\n    obj = [[r, c, g[r][c]] for r, c in sorted(piece[1])]\\n    tar = [[r, c, g[r][c]] for r, c in sorted(tgt[1])]\\n    if d > 8.5:\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"noop\\\", \\\"action\\\": action, \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj,\\n                \\\"target_pixels\\\": tar, \\\"expected_pixels\\\": [],\\n                \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"click is %.1f cells from the pawn, past the \\\"\\n                                \\\"~8-cell relocation reach\\\" % d,\\n                \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pawn \\\" + piece[0] + \\\" must stay put when out of reach\\\"}\\n    rows = range(max(0, rr - 3), min(len(g), rr + 4))\\n    cols = range(max(0, cc - 3), min(len(g[0]), cc + 4))\\n    exp = [[r, c, piece[0]] for r in rows for c in cols]\\n    return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"change\\\", \\\"action\\\": action, \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj,\\n            \\\"target_pixels\\\": tar, \\\"expected_pixels\\\": exp,\\n            \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"a MOUSE click within ~8 cells relocates the \\\"\\n                            \\\"selected pawn to the clicked neighbourhood\\\",\\n            \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pawn \\\" + piece[0] + \\\" relocation one step toward the \\\"\\n                     \\\"S/b target region\\\"}\\n\"}"

pc[0], cc - pc[1])\\n    obj = [[r, c, g[r][c]] for r, c in sorted(piece[1])]\\n    tar = [[r, c, g[r][c]] for r, c in sorted(tgt[1])]\\n    if d > 8.5:\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"noop\\\", \\\"action\\\": action, \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj,\\n                \\\"target_pixels\\\": tar, \\\"expected_pixels\\\": [],\\n                \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"click is %.1f cells from the pawn, past the \\\"\\n                                \\\"~8-cell relocation reach\\\" % d,\\n                \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pawn \\\" + piece[0] + \\\" must stay put when out of reach\\\"}\\n    rows = range(max(0, rr - 3), min(len(g), rr + 4))\\n    cols = range(max(0, cc - 3), min(len(g[0]), cc + 4))\\n    exp = [[r, c, piece[0]] for r in rows for c in cols]\\n    return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"change\\\", \\\"action\\\": action, \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj,\\n            \\\"target_pixels\\\": tar, \\\"expected_pixels\\\": exp,\\n            \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"a MOUSE click within ~8 cells relocates the \\\"\\n                            \\\"selected pawn to the clicked neighbourhood\\\",\\n            \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pawn \\\" + piece[0] + \\\" relocation one step toward the \\\"\\n                     \\\"S/b target region\\\"}\\n\"}"

pc[0], cc - pc[1])\\n    obj = [[r, c, g[r][c]] for r, c in sorted(piece[1])]\\n    tar = [[r, c, g[r][c]] for r, c in sorted(tgt[1])]\\n    if d > 8.5:\\n        return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"noop\\\", \\\"action\\\": action, \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj,\\n                \\\"target_pixels\\\": tar, \\\"expected_pixels\\\": [],\\n                \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"click is %.1f cells from the pawn, past the \\\"\\n                                \\\"~8-cell relocation reach\\\" % d,\\n                \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pawn \\\" + piece[0] + \\\" must stay put when out of reach\\\"}\\n    rows = range(max(0, rr - 3), min(len(g), rr + 4))\\n    cols = range(max(0, cc - 3), min(len(g[0]), cc + 4))\\n    exp = [[r, c, piece[0]] for r in rows for c in cols]\\n    return {\\\"mode\\\": \\\"change\\\", \\\"action\\\": action, \\\"object_pixels\\\": obj,\\n            \\\"target_pixels\\\": tar, \\\"expected_pixels\\\": exp,\\n            \\\"precondition\\\": \\\"a MOUSE click within ~8 cells relocates the \\\"\\n                            \\\"selected pawn to the clicked neighbourhood\\\",\\n            \\\"scope\\\": \\\"pawn \\\" + piece[0] + \\\" relocation one step toward the \\\"\\n                     \\\"S/b target region\\\"}\\n\"}"
